- **devops-pipeline**: Use RIGHT AFTER merging a PR to `main` to babysit the GitHub Actions CI/CD pipeline ("Prod Deployment"). It watches the run, and if a job fails it pulls the failed logs, pinpoints the root cause, classifies the failure as backend or frontend, and hands a precise fix brief to the `backend-dev` or `frontend-dev` agent, then re-watches the pipeline the fix triggers — looping until the pipeline is green. - **diagrammer**: Software cartographer. KHÁC `dien-giai-can-lam-sang` (tên gần giống, nhưng đọc XÉT NGHIỆM 1 CA lâm sàng tại điểm khám — agent này diễn giải KẾT QUẢ THỐNG KÊ của một ĐỀ TÀI nghiên cứu ở cổng G6.5). ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dien-giai-ket-qua.md))
- **diesel-infrastructure-mechanic**: Use this agent when you need infrastructure troubleshooting with a mechanic's intuition, Windows-first DevOps solutions, or when you need someone who understands systems at a visceral level. Diesel excels at diagnosing infrastructure problems through "feel" and mechanical sympathy, treating servers like vintage machines that need proper care. Perfect for complex debugging, performance issues, or when you need someone who can make legacy systems purr. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User has mysterious server performance issues. user: "Our servers are running slow but all the metrics look normal"… ([ejfox/dotfiles](https://github.com/ejfox/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diesel-infrastructure-mechanic.md) · ★1)
- **diet**: Nutrition advisor. Owner of meal plans, macros and shopping lists. Use PROACTIVELY when the user asks about diet, food, meals, protein, macros, a meal plan, meal prep or calories, or says '@diet'. Deliverable: a concrete meal plan with a shopping list, not a lecture on nutrition. 'Fuel, not punishment.' ([zmrlk/bOS](https://github.com/zmrlk/bOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diet.md) · ★14)

- **dieu-phoi-lam-sang**: Điều phối trọn một CA khám ngoại trú EBM theo 5 bước (Hỏi→Tìm→Thẩm định→Áp dụng→Theo dõi). Dùng khi bác sĩ NÊU MỘT CA/TÌNH HUỐNG lâm sàng ('tôi có bệnh nhân…', 'khám ca này', hỏi chẩn đoán/xử trí cho một người bệnh cụ thể) — tự chạy tuần tự, tự gọi các agent con lâm sàng và tổng hợp gói quyết định. KHÔNG dùng cho tổng quan y văn/bản thảo/dashboard. Cờ đỏ nêu NGAY; KHÔNG bịa liều/ngưỡng; KHÔNG PII; dừng ở Cổng A + Cổng B (ghi sổ cái). ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieu-phoi-lam-sang.md))
- **dieu-phoi-nghien-cuu**: Điều phối đề tài nghiên cứu y khoa từ ý tưởng đến gói phát hành qua G0–G10. Dùng khi nhà nghiên cứu nêu MỘT đề tài/câu hỏi nghiên cứu, hoặc chạy một chặng vòng đời. CHỈ CẦN TÊN/MÔ TẢ ĐỀ TÀI là tự march G0→G10, dừng ở 6 cổng cứng: đạo đức G2 · SAP G4 · khóa dữ liệu G5 · bình duyệt G8 · liêm chính tác giả G9 · PI khóa gói phát hành G10. KHÔNG bịa dữ liệu/phê duyệt; KHÔNG PII. ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieu-phoi-nghien-cuu.md))
- **diff-agent**: Deterministic scripted agent for diff artefact capture smoke tests. ([m-dwyer/ordin](https://github.com/m-dwyer/ordin/blob/HEAD/bundles/diff-capture/agents/diff-agent.md))
- **diff-analyst**: Reads a large working-tree diff and proposes how to split it into atomic commits — grouping hunks by the intent behind them, flagging secrets and contamination, and returning an ordered plan. Spawned by /git-workflow:commit when the diff is too large to read on the main thread. Read-only; proposes rather than commits. ([bobtat/claude-plugins](https://github.com/bobtat/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/git-workflow/agents/diff-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **diff-analyst**: Generates structured candidate defects from a diff. Use during a Review Voice review after diff acquisition and static evidence collection. Returns JSON candidates only, never prose. ([QuintinBotes/review-voice](https://github.com/QuintinBotes/review-voice/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-voice/agents/diff-analyst.md))
- **diff-analyzer**: 分析 Git 歷史，識別可能引入問題的提交，追蹤程式碼變更。 擅長從版本控制歷史中找出問題的引入時間點，分析變更對功能的影響。 使用時機： - "檢查最近的程式碼變更" - "找出可能引入這個 bug 的提交" - "比較正常版本和問題版本的差異" - "分析這個檔案的修改歷史" - "誰最近修改了這個功能" ([DennisLiuCk/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/DennisLiuCk/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/issue-review/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★27)
- **diff-analyzer**: Hallucination Detector — pre-reviewer gate between @Worker and @Reviewer. Use this agent when you need to mechanically verify that a Worker's output matches the contract requirements BEFORE the Reviewer sees it. Examples: - &lt;example&gt; Context: Worker completed a FILE_OPERATION contract and claims success. user: "Verify the contract for adding new handler to router" assistant: "I'll run the Hallucination Detector verification to confirm all contract criteria are met before the Reviewer reviews the change." &lt;/example&gt; - &lt;example&gt; Context: Worker claims a bug fix is complete and tests pass. user:… ([Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot](https://github.com/Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-analyzer**: Analyzes a git diff to categorize changes by type, identify high-risk modifications, and flag large changesets. Read-only. ([ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize](https://github.com/ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · 2 copies)
- **diff-auditor**: Final diff audit agent. Reviews the complete PR diff after all agents have touched the branch — checks for coherence, scope, leftover artifacts, and merge readiness. ([EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp](https://github.com/EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-auditor.md) · ★27)
- **diff-auditor**: Audit the current git diff against coding-standards.md - fix what's mechanical (non-English comments, stray debug output), report what needs human judgement (secrets, unlinked TODOs, ownership gaps, complexity budget). Use before a commit or a PR. ([kidp8479/42_hypertube](https://github.com/kidp8479/42_hypertube/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-auditor.md))
- **diff-auditor**: Audit the current git diff (security, secrets, non-English comments, stray TODOs) and return a list of findings. Use before a commit or a PR. ([kidp8479/ravito](https://github.com/kidp8479/ravito/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-auditor.md))
- **diff-blind-reviewer**: Plan-blind diff reviewer. Groups a diff by intent, orders by risk, and flags findings — reading existing repo code to catch misuse of unchanged functions, but never reading the plan. Requires a dispatch prompt with a ctx-prefixed diff and an output schema. ([matsuyoshi30/dotfiles](https://github.com/matsuyoshi30/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/diff-blind-reviewer.md))
- **diff-bug-agent**: Reviews PowerShell changes for bugs introduced by the diff itself. Reasons about contracts that changed (signatures, output types, error behaviour, pipeline semantics) and what that breaks for callers. The agent that uses Ring 1 context (callers, callees, tests) most heavily. ([andresharpe/pwsh-code-review-marketplace](https://github.com/andresharpe/pwsh-code-review-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/pwsh-code-review/agents/diff-bug-agent.md) · ★1)
- **diff-collector**: (내부용) /code-review 커맨드에서 호출되는 diff 수집 에이전트 ([kys0213/kys-claude-plugin](https://github.com/kys0213/kys-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/external-llm/agents/diff-collector.md))
- **diff-context-analyzer**: あなたは差分コンテキスト分析のスペシャリストです。マルチエージェントコードレビューのために、コミット履歴を収集し、差分パターンを分析して文脈を提供します。 ([godslew/enigma](https://github.com/godslew/enigma/blob/HEAD/plugins/enigma/skills/code-review-bahamut/agents/diff-context-analyzer.md))
- **diff-explorer**: Maps a git diff — what changed, why, and what risk areas to focus on. Invoked by full-review before handing off to reviewers. Read-only. ([noetic97/comictracker](https://github.com/noetic97/comictracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-fox-reviewer**: AI code reviewer that reviews local branch changes using git diff — never fetches from GitHub ([mthooyavan/diff-fox](https://github.com/mthooyavan/diff-fox/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-investigator**: Investigates uncommitted code changes (staged and unstaged) to produce a structured investigation report for review agents. Use as Phase 1 when reviewing local changes before commit. ([avovello/cc-plugins](https://github.com/avovello/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/diff-investigator.md) · ★1)
- **diff-narrator**: Analyzes code diffs and generates TTS-friendly narration explaining changes like a staff engineer ([dtran320/coderegon-trail](https://github.com/dtran320/coderegon-trail/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-narrator.md) · ★1)
- **diff-reader**: BASE_BRANCH: [Tên branch dùng làm baseline — ví dụ: main, develop, release/1.2, staging] ([hiepdnh/Agentic-Development-Lifecycle](https://github.com/hiepdnh/Agentic-Development-Lifecycle/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-reader.md) · ★6)
- **diff-reflex**: Lightning self-review subagent for uncommitted diffs. Flags CRITICAL issues only while coder is in-session. ([Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack](https://github.com/Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-reflex.md) · 2 copies)
- **diff-reviewer**: diff-review スキル専用のワーカーエージェント。スキルがレンズ・収集済み差分 manifest を prompt で渡して並列起動する。ユーザーからコードレビューを依頼された場合はこの agent を直接起動せず、必ず diff-review スキルを使うこと。コードの修正は行わず、指摘の報告のみ行う。 ([yasunori0418/skills](https://github.com/yasunori0418/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/git/diff-review/agents/diff-reviewer.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **diff-reviewer**: Review worker. Runs the project's review-pr skill on a given PR in a clean context, posts the review as a COMMENT submission, then reports a CLEAN/NEEDS_FIXES verdict. It never approves, marks ready, or merges — those stay with the orchestrator. Quality gate before the PR is merged — unattended or by a human. Spawned by the /developer orchestrator. Not for direct use. ([sgomez/developer-skills](https://github.com/sgomez/developer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/diff-reviewer.md) · ★7)
- **diff-reviewer**: Read-only sidecar reviewer for sealed diff packets; returns structured JSON findings only. ([mir3626/vibe-doctor](https://github.com/mir3626/vibe-doctor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **diff-reviewer**: Relit le diff de la branche courante dans un contexte frais, en LECTURE SEULE, et liste uniquement les régressions, oublis et bugs par rapport au plan/à l'intention. Ne modifie aucun fichier. À invoquer avant de clôturer une tâche d'implémentation. ([LeDREVM/LiikaDjaProcess](https://github.com/LeDREVM/LiikaDjaProcess/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 7 copies)
- **diff-reviewer**: Reviews the current working diff (vs upstream/main) against Chimaera's invariants — the daemon↔UI wire contract, auth-on-every-route, bounded RSS / no-blocking-fs-on-the-reactor, terminal-state-server-side, pinned agent protocols, verify-live. Read-only: it reports findings ranked by severity, it does NOT fix. Use before opening a PR, or to sanity-check a branch. ([martinappberg/chimaera](https://github.com/martinappberg/chimaera/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-reviewer**: Reviews the current branch's diff against main in a fresh context, seeing only the change and this repository's own rules — never the reasoning that produced it. Use before opening a pull request, or when asked for a review of the work so far. Reports findings; does not fix them and does not block. ([tomo-chan/panemux](https://github.com/tomo-chan/panemux/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-reviewer**: Adversarial read-only review of a branch diff, hunting for defects the tests would not catch — wrong-layer placement, broken contracts, security and multi-tenancy leaks, silent no-ops. Dispatch after ws-verifier passes, before the PR is opened. ([FracktalWorks/CommandCenter](https://github.com/FracktalWorks/CommandCenter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-reviewer.md))
- **diff-reviewer**: Deeply inspects a git diff for bugs, security issues, and quality problems. Invoke for careful line-by-line review of changed code, especially before a PR goes to a human reviewer. - **divergence-convergence**: Implements the structured divergence-convergence thinking cycle. Activates when the agent needs to explore multiple solution paths before committing to one, when a task benefits from "thinking outside the box", or when the user explicitly requests brainstorming, exploration, or creative solutions. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User asks to design an API architecture user: "Design an API architecture for our service" assistant: "Entering divergence phase — generating multiple architecture patterns before converging on the optimal design..." &lt;commentary&gt;Instead of jumping to the first architectu… ([Ewallyw/Agent-cognitive-flexibility](https://github.com/Ewallyw/Agent-cognitive-flexibility/blob/HEAD/agents/divergence-convergence.md) · ★1)
- **divergence-investigator**: Decides, with evidence, whether a hand-port (sge/ssg) divergence from upstream java was JUSTIFIED — by reading the reference repo's git history, docs/ and .rescale/data/*.tsv — and names the spelling it should take in Baltic Porter. Read-only; one divergence row per invocation, run for every row the `divergence` lane produces. ([kubuszok/balticporter](https://github.com/kubuszok/balticporter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/divergence-investigator.md))
- **divergence-prober**: Spec-gauntlet role — Underdetermination lens. Constructs two materially different implementations that BOTH fully satisfy the spec, then reports every point where they diverge. Each divergence is a decision the spec left to chance; each gets a one-line pin the spec can adopt. Returns a single-perspective response, NOT a synthesis. Use when a draft spec needs to be tested for what it fails to determine — the pass that catches the gaps a careful reader will not notice, because the spec reads fine and simply doesn't say. Distinct from the consistency audit (which finds what the spec contradicts)… ([trailhead-ai/trailhead](https://github.com/trailhead-ai/trailhead/blob/HEAD/tools/craft/plugins/craft/agents/divergence-prober.md) · ★3)
- **divergent**: Generates candidate ideas under a fixed persona and a fixed creative operator. Multiple instances run in parallel each round, each with a distinct operator and persona. - **divergent-explorer**: Generatore di idee divergenti. Produce 30-50 angoli/idee senza giudizio, organizzate per categorie. - **diversity-planner**: Reads a set of idea candidates, says what they have in common, and names three to six fields whose literature would make the set less self-similar — each with a query in that field's own words and the home vocabulary blocked. Names fields; never generates ideas. Dispatched by the ideas skill, once per run. ([rbh227/research-bearings](https://github.com/rbh227/research-bearings/blob/HEAD/agents/diversity-planner.md) · ★1)
- **divi5-accessibility**: Use this agent when reviewing Divi 5.11 CSS for accessibility issues. Checks color contrast, focus indicators (with Divi 5.3 pseudo-class editing as a no-CSS alternative for form fields), touch targets, reduced motion support, semantic elements, and ARIA attribute recommendations. Activates when writing CSS for interactive elements or when the user mentions accessibility, WCAG, ADA, or a11y. ([cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit](https://github.com/cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit/blob/HEAD/plugins/divi5-toolkit/agents/divi5-accessibility.md) · ★61)
- **divi5-error-learner**: Use this agent when the user pastes Divi error messages, console errors from Divi pages, or describes CSS issues they encountered in Divi 5. Analyzes the error, provides solutions, and updates the plugin's knowledge base to prevent future occurrences. ([cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit](https://github.com/cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit/blob/HEAD/plugins/divi5-toolkit/agents/divi5-error-learner.md) · ★61)
- **divi5-performance**: Use this agent when auditing Divi 5 site performance, diagnosing slow pages, improving Core Web Vitals (LCP, INP, CLS), reducing render-blocking CSS, debugging font/image loading, configuring Divi's Critical CSS / Dynamic CSS / Inline Stylesheets, or resolving conflicts between Divi and performance plugins (WP Rocket, LiteSpeed, Autoptimize, Perfmatters). Activates when the user mentions performance, speed, PageSpeed, Lighthouse, Core Web Vitals, LCP, INP, CLS, slow loading, render-blocking, or asks why a Divi page is slow. ([cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit](https://github.com/cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit/blob/HEAD/plugins/divi5-toolkit/agents/divi5-performance.md) · ★61)
- **divi5-researcher**: Use this agent to research the latest Divi 5 updates, features, and compatibility changes. Triggers automatically when plugin knowledge is stale (&gt;7 days since last research) or on-demand when user asks about Divi 5 updates, new features, or compatibility questions. Knows the 5.3–5.11 changelog (pseudo-class editing, CF7 Styler, Variable Generators, Aspect Ratio/Framing, Gradient Variables, Workspaces, Variable Fonts, CSS Grid Editor, Loop Filters, and the 5.6–5.11 new modules) and checks victorduse.com/divi-5-changelog/ for updates. ([cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit](https://github.com/cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit/blob/HEAD/plugins/divi5-toolkit/agents/divi5-researcher.md) · ★61)
- **divi5-validator**: Use this agent after writing or editing CSS files to validate Divi 5.11 compatibility. Triggers on CSS file changes and checks for button specificity issues, numbered selectors, missing !important on Divi overrides, and other compatibility problems. Knows about 5.3 form field selectors and pseudo-class editing, 5.5 Aspect Ratio/Framing, and Composable Settings opportunities. ([cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit](https://github.com/cjsimon2/Divi5-ToolKit/blob/HEAD/plugins/divi5-toolkit/agents/divi5-validator.md) · ★61)
